## Consequential corrections

- Scan 28, asterisk note: remaining verification: et qu'il \unsure{n'a pas} d'éléments d'ordre fini! Sinon, se borner dans le cor. au cas de fibres successives non complètes. / . \ill{} \uncertain{d'Artin} \ill{} \uncertain{de borner} \ill{} \uncertain{dans le cas des fibrations successives} \ill{} → et qu'il n'a pas \unsure{d'éléments|d'élts} d'ordre fini! Sinon, se borner dans le cor. \unsure{au|aux} cas \unsure{de|des} fibres successives non complètes.. The lower marginal lines visibly carry et qu’il, n’a pas, d’éléments d’ordre, fini!, Sinon, se borner dans, le cor. au cas de fibres, successives non complètes. The initial apostrophe after n and the descending p of pas are distinct. Fibres has the tall f then i-b-r-e-s, without the extra letters of fibrations. Blind B13: the compressed éléments/élts sequence and the tiny endings of au(x) and de(s) remain locally doubtful; n’a pas is still resolved.
- Scan 29, relation between the two base-changed maps, blind B10: $\pi_1(\overline f)$, $\pi_1(\overline g)$ / $\pi_1(\overline f),\pi_1(\overline g)$ → $\pi_1(\overline f)\ne\pi_1(\overline g)$. The sign has two distinct short horizontal bars and one long crossing stroke that continues below them. It is a narrow not-equal sign, not a comma. The full formula and the small sign detail were both viewed; mathematical expectation did not decide the relation.

- Scan 51, L19, sign between sur and un K; M1127: $\unsure{\forall}$ un $K$ / un $K$ → $\unsure{\forall|q}$ un $K$. A visible sign remains between sur and un: a small upper-left loop or bowl touches a long descending stroke with dark retracing and a lower return. C’s universal-sign reading remains possible, while the loop and descender also support a q-like alternative. There is no secure independent cancellation stroke. The sign is retained, not expanded or omitted.
- Scan 52, First base-topos symbol: X_{\mathrm{et}} / S_{\text{et}} → X_{\mathrm{et}}. The large initial letter has two crossing diagonals: it is X, not the curved S used later. The written X is retained despite the base scheme being named S.

(\unsure{géométriquem.})} / \add{schéma \uncertain{géom.\ connexe} lisse de t.f.} → \inserted{schéma connexe lisse de t.f. \inserted{\unsure{géométriquem.|géométriquem}}}.
